\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(2)}}{{\geqslant}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{1+\delta}}}\right)^{2t-1}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle W(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0}),v}}\right\rangle^{2t}-O\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+\frac{1}{\delta}}}\right)^{2t-1}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\hat{W}-W}}\right)(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0}),v}}\right\rangle^{2t},

where in (1) we used that, by
Lemma

A.6
,
\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{18.66075pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-18.66075pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[18.66075pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-18.66075pt\raisebox{3.32222pt}{\makebox[18.66075pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{A,B}$}}}$}}}A^{2t}\leqslant(1+\delta)^{2t-1}(A+B)^{2t}+(1+\frac{1}{\delta})^{2t-1}B^{2t}
, so
\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{18.66075pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-18.66075pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[18.66075pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-18.66075pt\raisebox{3.32222pt}{\makebox[18.66075pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{A,B}$}}}$}}}(A+B)^{2t}\geqslant(\frac{1}{1+\delta})^{2t-1}A^{2t}-(\frac{1+\frac{1}{\delta}}{1+\delta})^{2t-1}B^{2t}
. In (2) we assumed that
\delta=O(1)
, which will be the case for our choice.

Now take
\delta=\frac{\eta}{100t}
. Then
(1+\delta)^{2t-1}\leqslant 1+\eta
and
\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{1+\delta}}}\right)^{2t-1}\geqslant 1-\eta
for
\eta
small.

For the second term in both bounds, we use that

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{11.00691pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{1.96112pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{v}$}}}$}}}\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\hat{W}-W}}\right)(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0}),v}}\right\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\hat{W}W^{-1}-I_{d}}}\right)W(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0}),v}}\right\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|I_{d}-\hat{W}W^{-1}}}\right\|^{2t}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|W(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0})}}\right\|^{2t}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}}}\right\|^{2t}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|W(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0})}}\right\|^{2t}.

By
Lemma

A.20
and
Lemma

A.26
, with probability
1-\varepsilon
,

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}}}\right\|\leqslant\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\eta}{tp_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d}}}\right)^{O(1)}.

By
Lemma

A.24
, with probability
1-\varepsilon
,

\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|W(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0})}}\right\|^{2t}\leqslant(p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d)^{O(t)}.

Then the second term in both bounds becomes

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{11.00691pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{1.96112pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{v}$}}}$}}}O\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+\frac{1}{\delta}}}\right)^{2t-1}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\hat{W}-W}}\right)(\bm{y}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{0}),v}}\right\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle\qquad\leqslant O\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{100t}{\eta}}}\right)^{2t-1}\cdot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\eta}{tp_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d}}}\right)^{O(t)}\cdot(p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d)^{O(t)}\leqslant\eta.

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6.7
.

We have

\displaystyle|\langle W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle-\langle\hat{W}(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle(W-\hat{W})(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle(I_{d}-\hat{W}W^{-1})W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle|

\displaystyle\leqslant\|I_{d}-\hat{W}W^{-1}\|\cdot\|W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0})\|

\displaystyle=\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}\|\cdot\|W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0})\|.

By
Lemma

A.20
and
Lemma

A.26
, with probability
1-\varepsilon
,

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}}}\right\|\leqslant\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\eta}{p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}}}}\right)^{O(1)}.

Note that
W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0})=\mu_{i}-\mu_{j}=\langle\mu_{i}-\mu_{j},u\rangle u
, so
\|W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0})\|=|\langle\mu_{i}-\mu_{j},u\rangle|
. Using that
\sum_{i=1}^{k}p_{i}\langle\mu_{i},u\rangle^{2}\leqslant 1
, we have that
\sum_{i=1}^{k}\langle\mu_{i},u\rangle^{2}\leqslant p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}
, so
\langle\mu_{i},u\rangle^{2}\leqslant p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}
, so
|\langle\mu_{i}-\mu_{j},u\rangle|\leqslant 2\sqrt{p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}}
. Then
\|W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0})\|\leqslant 2\sqrt{p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}}
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle|\langle W(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle-\langle\hat{W}(\mu_{i}^{0}-\mu_{j}^{0}),v\rangle|

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\eta}{p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}}}}\right)^{O(1)}\cdot 2\sqrt{p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}}\leqslant\eta.

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6.8
.

We have

\displaystyle\|v^{\top}W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}-v^{\top}\hat{W}(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|v^{\top}(W-\hat{W})(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|v^{\top}(I_{d}-\hat{W}W^{-1})W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leqslant\|I_{d}-\hat{W}W^{-1}\|\cdot\|W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}\|\cdot\|W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|.

By
Lemma

A.20
and
Lemma

A.26
, with probability
1-\varepsilon
,

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|I_{d}-(\hat{W}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y}^{0})\hat{W}^{\top})^{1/2}}}\right\|\leqslant\eta.

Note that, by
Lemma

A.27
,
W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}=Q(\Sigma^{0})^{-1/2}(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}=Q
for an orthogonal matrix
Q
. We have
\|Q\|=1
, so
\|W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|=1
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|v^{\top}W(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}-v^{\top}\hat{W}(\Sigma^{0})^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta.

∎

A.9
Proofs deferred from Section
7

Proof of
Lemma

7.4
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{11.00691pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{1.96112pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{v}$}}}$}}}\hat{\mathbb{E}}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}-\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{E}}\langle\bm{y}^{\otimes 2t},v^{\otimes 2t}\rangle-\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y}^{\otimes 2t},v^{\otimes 2t}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\hat{\mathbb{E}}\bm{y}^{\otimes 2t}-\mathbb{E}\bm{y}^{\otimes 2t},v^{\otimes 2t}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t},(vv^{\top})^{\otimes t}\rangle.

We now bound
\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}
. Define

E=\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2}\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2}\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2})^{\otimes t}.

By
Lemma

A.23
, with probability
1-d^{2t}\delta
, we have that

\|E\|_{F}=\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2}\bm{y})^{\otimes 2t}-\mathbb{E}(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{-1/2}\bm{y})^{\otimes 2t}\|\leqslant\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\delta}}(p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d)^{O(t)}.

For
n\geqslant(p_{\mathrm{min}}^{-1}d)^{O(t)}\eta^{-2}\varepsilon^{-1}
this term is
\eta
with probability at least
1-\varepsilon
. In this case
\|E\|\leqslant\|E\|_{F}\leqslant\eta
, so

-\eta\cdot\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{\otimes t}\preceq(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{1/2})^{\otimes t}E(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{1/2})^{\otimes t}\preceq\eta\cdot\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{\otimes t}.

We observe the connection between
\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}
and
E
:

\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}=(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{1/2})^{\otimes t}E(\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})^{1/2})^{\otimes t}.

Using this and using that
\operatorname{cov}(\bm{y})\preceq\mathbb{E}\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}
, we finally obtain that

-\eta\cdot\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}\preceq\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}\preceq\eta\cdot\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}.

Then

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{11.00691pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{1.96112pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{v}$}}}$}}}\hat{\mathbb{E}}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}-\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle=\langle\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t},(vv^{\top})^{\otimes t}\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\cdot\langle\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t},(vv^{\top})^{\otimes t}\rangle

\displaystyle=\eta\cdot\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}

and

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-0.23894pt]{11.00691pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{-6.47223pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{2t}$}}}\hskip-11.00691pt\raisebox{1.96112pt}{\makebox[11.00691pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{v}$}}}$}}}\hat{\mathbb{E}}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}-\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}

\displaystyle=\langle\hat{\mathbb{E}}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t}-\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t},(vv^{\top})^{\otimes t}\rangle

\displaystyle\geqslant-\eta\cdot\langle\mathbb{E}(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top})^{\otimes t},(vv^{\top})^{\otimes t}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\eta\cdot\mathbb{E}\langle\bm{y},v\rangle^{2t}.

The conclusion follows.
∎
